1	---
2	created: 2026-06-11
3	title: FUSE release() reports success then can silently lose data
4	area: desktop-fuse
5	severity: high
6	files:
7	  - crates/fuse/src/read_ops.rs
8	  - crates/fuse/src/platform/windows/write_ops.rs
9	  - packages/sdk/src/queue.rs
10	---
11	
12	## Problem
13	
14	`flush` is a no-op (`read_ops.rs:852-854`). `release` replies OK to the OS, spawns
15	a detached upload thread, then immediately zeroizes and deletes the local temp file
16	(`read_ops.rs:835-848`). Upload failure in that thread is `log::error!`-only
17	(`read_ops.rs:832-834`). Same pattern on Windows (`windows/write_ops.rs:821-865`).
18	
19	After the OS-acknowledged close, the only copy of the data is in-memory ciphertext
20	in a detached thread. A crash, a kill, or an upload failure = silent permanent data
21	loss after the user (and the OS) were told the write succeeded.
22	
23	Severity: data loss with false durability ack.
24	
25	## Solution
26	
27	TBD — key considerations:
28	
29	- Do not delete/zeroize the temp file until the remote commit (IPFS add + pin +
30	  IPNS publish) is durably confirmed.
```

18	hooks). A caller that forgets the base snapshot silently hits the union-fallback
19	`console.warn` path where deletes can resurrect — e.g. useFileVersions.ts calls
20	without baseChildren. The shared-write functions also still return a now-redundant
21	`updatedChildren` (the stale pre-merge set) alongside `publishedChildren`, a foot-gun
22	that invites callers to consume the wrong one.

11	## Problem
12	
13	Phase 44 added two independent 409-conflict retry engines that duplicate the same

17	- `updateFolderMetadataAndPublish` (folder/index.ts): a 4-attempt `for` loop with

19	- `updateFileMetadata` (file/index.ts): a hand-unrolled 2-attempt try/catch with
20	  no backoff, merging via `mergeVersions` + loser-as-version.
21	
22	They have already drifted (4 vs 2 attempts; backoff vs none). A future change to
23	retry count, backoff, or sequence handling must be made twice and is easy to apply

3	title: updateSharedFile discards prunedCids from updateFileMetadata causing pin leak

9	
10	## Problem
11	
12	After phase 44, `updateFileMetadata` returns `prunedCids` — version-history CIDs that
13	overflowed the per-file cap and should be unpinned. The owner path
14	(useFileOperations.ts) consumes and unpins them, but `updateSharedFile`
15	(shared-write.ts) calls `updateFileMetadata` and throws the entire return value away
16	(bare call, no destructure) with a "deferred leak" comment. Every shared-file update
17	that prunes a version therefore leaks pinned storage that is never unpinned.
18	
19	This is a correctness / storage-cost issue, not just cleanup. Surfaced by `/simplify`
20	(2026-06-14) but flagged as `/code-review`-grade rather than a quality cleanup, so it
21	was not auto-fixed in that pass.
22	
23	## Solution
24	
25	TBD — key considerations:
26	
27	- Consume `prunedCids` in `updateSharedFile` and unpin them (mirror
28	  useFileOperations.ts), OR move the unpin-of-pruned-CIDs INSIDE the shared publish
```

   145	## Accumulated Context
   146	
   147	### Key Decisions
   148	
   149	See PROJECT.md Key Decisions table for full list with outcomes.
   150	
   151	Recent for v1.1:
   152	
   153	- make_temp_queue in crates/sdk/src/queue.rs uses pid+counter (not tid+counter) to prevent inter-run temp dir collisions (Phase 45 P01 Rule-1 fix)
   154	- T-45-07 uses root-shortcut path (folder_ipns_name==root_ipns_name) for deterministic resolve_folder_key test without network; marked for #15 extension
   155	- T-45-08 placed in crates/fuse/src/lib.rs (not apps/desktop) to keep characterization tests co-located with merge_folder_children under test
   156	- Network-first with self-hosted Someguy + DB fallback adopted as IPNS resolution strategy (revised from DB-first during Phase 19 context -- see 19-SCOPING_RATIONALE.md #1)
   157	- rootFolderKey DB copy kept as permanent fallback (never drop column, IPFS copy for recovery independence)
   158	- BYO-IPFS affects pinning only, all IPNS publishes still route through CipherBox API
   159	- PERF requirements split across Phase 18 (server-side, pre-change) and Phase 22 (client + load testing, post-change)
   160	- IPFS/IPNS histogram buckets: 1ms-30s exponential (14 buckets); republish batch: 1s-120s (10 buckets)
   161	- Source label (db/network) only for resolve operations; empty string for pin/cat/publish
   162	- Alloy scrapes Kubo directly via Docker internal network (ipfs:5001), not proxied through API
   163	- Kubo Health dashboard panels use fallback Go runtime metrics alongside libp2p metrics pending post-deploy verification
   164	- IPNS-specific histograms: resolve 50ms-30s, publish 100ms-60s with source/outcome labels
   165	- Null resolve results (not found) excluded from IPNS histogram observations
   166	- Used axios-functions orval client for @cipherbox/api-client (plain functions, no React deps)
   167	- sdk-core IPFS ops use direct axios/fetch (not api-client) for upload progress; IPNS ops use api-client generated functions
   168	- Bin/share operations take explicit context objects (BinOperationContext, ShareOperationContext) instead of Zustand stores
   169	- Share module accepts callback functions for API calls to stay transport-decoupled
   170	- Moved @cipherbox/core from dependencies to devDependencies in crypto (test-only cross-package assertions)
   171	- Kubo pebbleds datastore (LSM-tree) configured via IPFS_PROFILE=server,pebbleds; requires fresh volume on deploy
   172	- SDK concurrent pins require pebbleds datastore (synergistic); concurrent pins alone cause regression at 50 clients
   173	- Combined per-task commits into single commit due to pre-commit hook requiring api-client regeneration with entity/dto/controller changes
   174	- Desktop root folder detected by inode::ROOT_INO at publish call sites (simpler than modifying build_folder_metadata return type)
   175	- Desktop initialize_vault produces v2 blob for new users from day one (not just on migration)
   176	- decrypt_metadata_from_ipfs_public transparently handles both v1 JSON and v2 binary blobs
   177	- VaultExportDto returns only rootIpnsName and derivationMethod (crypto columns dropped)
   178	- Recovery tool IPNS resolution uses gateway /ipns/ HEAD request with redirect following (most reliable without API dependency)
   179	- fetchAndDecryptMetadata handles both v1 JSON and v2 binary blobs transparently for folder sync
   180	- Zero-crypto vault schema: server stores only ownerPublicKey and rootIpnsName, all crypto material lives exclusively in IPFS v2 blobs
   181	- DB crypto columns (encrypted_root_folder_key, encrypted_root_ipns_private_key, migrated_at) fully dropped — no fallback paths
   182	- PinningProvider interface: KuboProvider uses Basic auth, PsaProvider uses Bearer auth, matching each protocol's native auth model
   183	- PsaProvider.pin() throws intentionally; pinByCid() is the correct PSA workflow (CID-reference-only protocol)
   184	- Connection test uses sequential probe: Kubo /api/v0/id first, then PSA /pins, with 10s timeout per probe
   185	- CID registration gated to BYO users only via ForbiddenException (non-BYO users cannot bypass upload relay)
   186	- Advisory quota: checkQuota() always true for BYO, getQuota() includes advisory boolean flag for UI display
   187	- pinFn injection pattern: optional pinFn parameter on sdkCore.uploadFile() replaces addToIpfs when BYO mode active
   188	- External+Kubo bypasses CipherBox entirely; external+PSA uses relay for CID only; dual does both with best-effort secondary
   189	- PsaProvider.pinByCid() accessed via cast in client.ts (PSA-specific, not on PinningProvider interface)
   190	- Migration uses existing BullMQ pattern with pin-migration queue name; TEE decrypts ECIES-encrypted provider configs in-enclave with epoch key
   191	- SSRF protection on TEE migration: validates URL structure (HTTPS-only, no private IPs) and DNS resolution (rebinding check)
   192	- BYO config stored as encrypted IPNS entry using rootFolderKey — no server-side credential storage (zero-knowledge preserved)
   193	- Dedicated IPNS key derived via HKDF with context string byo-ipfs-config from vault keypair
   194	- BYO benchmark execution (21-07 Task 4) deferred — requires external IPFS provider infrastructure; test scenarios ready to run when provider available
   195	- BYO config loaded at login via IPNS resolve with graceful fallback to cipherbox-only mode
   196	- Source unpin is best-effort and non-fatal after verified CID transfer to destination
   197	- Cargo workspace with centralized deps at repo root; cipherbox-crypto crate as foundation for all Rust SDK extraction
   198	- Module re-export pattern in desktop crypto/mod.rs preserves all existing crate::crypto::\* paths without touching call sites
   199	- cipherbox-core crate layered on cipherbox-crypto: folder, file, bin, vault_blob, ipns, registry, decrypt, error modules
   200	- File module re-exports FileMetadata types from folder.rs (shared AES encryption context with parent folder key)
   201	- decrypt module moved from fuse to crypto re-export (domain logic, not FUSE-specific)
   202	- Hand-structured API client crate rather than openapi-generator (modest API surface, proven code, no Java/Docker CI dependency)
   203	- critical-section std feature required for standalone ecies linking (Tauri provides it in desktop builds)
   204	- Shared test vectors in tests/vectors/ JSON files loadable by both Rust and TypeScript for CI parity gates
   205	- SyncDaemon uses Arc<dyn Fn(SyncStatus)> generic callback instead of Tauri AppHandle for testability
   206	- Desktop api/client.rs re-exports cipherbox_api_client::ApiClient as type alias to unify types across modules
   207	- Desktop AppState wraps Arc<KeyState> from SDK; all key material accessed via state.sdk.\*
   208	- Keychain operations kept as desktop-specific keychain.rs module (not in api-client crate)
   209	- Desktop api/ and crypto/ directories fully removed; all imports use workspace crates directly
   210	- CI parity gate uses needs.changes.outputs.src (not nonexistent packages) for trigger condition
   211	- Desktop-e2e binary paths updated to target/debug/ to match workspace cargo build output
   212	- PinataProvider uses dual base URLs: uploads.pinata.cloud (fixed) for upload, api.pinata.cloud (configurable) for management
   213	- pinWithMode treats Pinata like Kubo: direct upload bypasses CipherBox relay entirely
   214	- Connection test probe order updated: Kubo -> Pinata -> PSA; pinata.cloud URLs skip Kubo probe
   215	- BYO Pinata baselines: pin p50=2.0s (+47% vs local Kubo), tail latency p99 13.5% better, 98% CipherBox API load reduction per file
   216	- perf.ts PERF_ENABLED evaluated once at module load (zero overhead in production); **CIPHERBOX_PERF** global for opt-in production debugging
   217	- Load test thresholds set at 2-3x observed baselines; spike test most generous (15s/15%); vitest expect() for CI failure on breach
   218	- Write-share authorization in upsertFolderIpns falls through to create-new-entry when no write share found (preserves backward compat for owner first publish)
   219	- TEE enrollFolder uses existing.userId (FolderIpns owner) for write-share publishes, not authenticated userId
   220	- Per-file IPNS records created for shared uploads (same as owner uploads) instead of empty fileMetaIpnsName PoC shortcut
   221	- File IPNS private key dual-wrapped: owner key in FilePointer, recipient key in share_keys (keyType: file-ipns)
   222	- addShareKeys API relaxed to allow write-share recipients to add keys to their own share
   223	- TextEditorDialog has separate shared file save path via onSaveSharedFile callback
   224	- Shared file download/view falls back to fileKeyEncrypted from metadata when no share_key exists
   225	- FilePointer resolution uses FileMetadata directly (no separate ResolvedFileMetadata struct)
   226	- FilePointer resolution scoped to parent folder via get_unresolved_file_pointers_for_parent() to avoid wrong-folder-key decryption
   227	- FilePointer async resolution: 500ms base \* 2^attempt exponential backoff (1s, 2s, 4s) with 3 retries
   228	- Removed custom dstack-sdk.d.ts since @phala/dstack-sdk@0.5.7 ships own TypeScript types
   229	- Defensive CVM key derivation handles both key (v0.5+) and asUint8Array (legacy) SDK return types
   230	- TEE worker Prometheus metrics use `cipherbox_tee_*` prefix for Grafana dashboard coexistence with API metrics
   231	- TEE worker structured JSON logger has zero external dependencies (JSON.stringify to stdout/stderr)
   232	
   233	### Roadmap Evolution
   234	
   235	- Phase 19.1 inserted after Phase 19: Extract core crypto SDK as shared package (URGENT)
   236	- Phase 19.2 inserted after Phase 19: IPFS Upload Performance Optimization (URGENT) — concurrent pins, Kubo worker tuning, pin batching to address ~95% bottleneck in upload path identified by Phase 19 baselines
   237	- Phase 23 added: Rust SDK Extraction — extract shared cipherbox-core crate, replace duplicated logic in desktop FUSE code, enable unit testing parity with TypeScript
   238	- Phase 27 added: Writable Shares (PoC) — extend read-only sharing to read-write using existing server-coordinated conflict resolution
   239	- Phase 36 added: Refactor upload progress in web app, to an inline progress display and remove the popup upload progress
   240	- Phase 37 added: Parallel batch upload pipeline — replace sequential per-file upload loop with parallel encrypt+pin and single folder metadata update
   241	- Phase 41 added: Package and app versioning and release cycles
   242	- Phase 42 added: API unpin integrity — ownership check, cross-user refcount, quota decrement (audit gap closure, todos 2026-06-11)
   243	- Phase 43 added: FUSE write durability — persisted upload journal, mkdir orphan fix (audit gap closure, todos 2026-06-11)
   244	- Phase 44 added: IPNS conflict handling — merge-on-409, file CAS (audit gap closure, todo 2026-06-11)
   245	- Phase 45 added: Desktop FUSE write-durability cleanup — Rust hygiene refactors + test coverage for phase 43/44 journal+replay (todos #11, #12, #14, #15, #18, #19, #20); excludes data-loss bugs #7/#8/#17
   246

   250	- Recovery tool subfolder recovery limited by IPNS DHT propagation (root-level fully operational; per-file IPNS records may not be resolvable if not propagated — architectural limitation, not a bug)

  "new_string": "- [x] 45-06-PLAN.md — Consolidate fuser/winfsp journal write paths into journal_helpers (#11)\n\n### Phase 46: Desktop FUSE data-loss bugs + replay hardening\n\n**Goal:** Close the desktop FUSE write-durability work that Phase 45 explicitly deferred — the three known data-loss bugs (mkdir orphan on parent-publish conflict, release() false-durability ack, stale-mount recovery on crash), the two replay-path hardening follow-ups from PR #491, and the remaining read_ops/write_ops + journal_helpers test coverage (Phase 45 Tier 2). Behavior-changing: these are correctness/durability fixes, not hygiene.\n**Requirements:** (1) mkdir must durably retry the parent publish on conflict instead of warn-only, so the new child folder is never orphaned remotely; (2) release()/flush must not ack the OS or zeroize+delete the local temp file until the remote commit is durably confirmed or the write is journaled for replay; (3) Linux startup must auto-recover a stale/disconnected FUSE mount (EEXIST/ENOTCONN) instead of failing and notifying; (4) park legacy empty `file_meta_ipns_name` replay entries instead of publishing an empty FilePointer; (5) use a strict (cache-bypassing) IPNS resolve in replay classification so transient failures retain the entry; (6) add the read_ops/write_ops handler test harness (unblock the fuser `ReplySender` limitation) plus the `journal_helpers` builder tests. Preserve all existing crash-recovery semantics.\n**Depends on:** Phase 45\n**Plans:** 0 plans (not yet planned)\n\nScope (captured todos):\n\n- [ ] FUSE mkdir orphans the new folder when parent publish conflicts (bug, high) — `2026-06-11-fuse-mkdir-parent-publish-orphan.md`\n- [ ] FUSE release() reports success then can silently lose data (bug, high) — `2026-06-11-fuse-release-data-loss-before-remote-commit.md`\n- [ ] Recover stale FUSE mount after crash on Linux startup (bug) — `2026-06-14-recover-stale-fuse-mount-after-crash-on-linux-startup.md`\n- [ ] Park legacy empty `file_meta_ipns_name` replay entries instead of empty FilePointer (PR #491 follow-up) — `2026-06-15-replay-empty-file-meta-ipns-name-publishes-empty-filepointer.md`\n- [ ] Use a strict (cache-bypassing) IPNS resolve in replay classification (PR #491 follow-up) — `2026-06-15-replay-resolve-ipns-strict-resolve-path-no-cache-fallback.md`\n- [ ] Improve Phase-43 rust write-durability test coverage — Tier 2 read_ops/write_ops harness + journal_helpers builders (Tier 1 landed in Phase 45) — `2026-06-14-improve-phase-43-rust-test-coverage.md`\n\n### Phase 47: SDK folder-state and publish-path consolidation\n\n**Goal:** Pay down the Phase-44 SDK structural debt surfaced by `/simplify` and `/code-review` — one owner for folder state, one CAS-retry engine shared by file and folder publishes, encapsulated child bookkeeping, and the `prunedCids` pin-leak fix on the shared-file path. Mostly refactor, plus one correctness fix (pin leak).\n**Requirements:** (1) Unify folder-state ownership so the web Zustand `useFolderStore` and the SDK client `folderTree` can no longer drift — treat IPNS `sequenceNumber` as the version clock and reconcile `folderTree` before sdk-core publishes; (2) unify the duplicated file/folder 409-CAS-retry loops into one `publishWithCas` helper in sdk-core so retry/backoff/sequence handling lives in one place; (3) encapsulate the `baseChildren`/`publishedChildren` snapshot-and-adopt ceremony inside `updateFolderMetadataAndPublish` so the ~14 call sites can't forget the base snapshot and resurrect deletes; (4) consume `prunedCids` in `updateSharedFile` and unpin them to stop the shared-file storage pin leak.\n**Depends on:** Phase 44\n**Plans:** 0 plans (not yet planned)\n\nScope (captured todos):\n\n- [ ] Unify folder-state ownership in the SDK client (medium) — `2026-06-14-unify-folder-state-ownership-in-sdk-client.md`\n- [ ] Unify file and folder IPNS CAS-retry into one publishWithCas helper (sdk-core) — `2026-06-14-unify-file-and-folder-ipns-cas-retry-into-one-publishwithcas.md`\n- [ ] Folder writes leak baseChildren/publishedChildren bookkeeping to call sites — `2026-06-14-folder-writes-leak-basechildren-and-publishedchildren-bookke.md`\n- [ ] updateSharedFile discards prunedCids from updateFileMetadata causing pin leak (medium) — `2026-06-14-updatesharedfile-discards-prunedcids-from-updatefilemetadata.md`\n\n---\n\n_Roadmap created: 2026-03-07_"
}

id: toolu_01DqZYb5f4E77x4d6tRrQMif
```
1	---
2	created: 2026-06-14T18:49:51.903Z
3	title: Unify folder-state ownership in the SDK client
4	area: sdk
5	severity: medium
6	files:
7	  - packages/sdk/src/client.ts
8	  - apps/web/src/lib/sdk-provider.ts
9	  - apps/web/src/hooks/useFileOperations.ts
10	  - apps/web/src/hooks/useFileVersions.ts
11	  - apps/web/src/stores/folder.store.ts
12	---
13	
14	## Problem
15	
16	Folder state (children + IPNS `sequenceNumber`) is duplicated across two stores
17	that can silently drift:
18	
19	1. Web Zustand `useFolderStore` (apps/web) — drives the UI.
20	2. SDK client `folderTree` (packages/sdk `client.ts`) — used by SDK-routed
21	   mutations (`deleteToBin`, `move`, `rename`, folder create) and by the headless
22	   desktop FUSE mount.
23	
24	`ensureFolderRegistered` (apps/web/src/lib/sdk-provider.ts) treats the `folderTree`
25	as authoritative and historically **no-opped once a folder was registered**. But
26	some web paths publish folder metadata **directly via sdk-core**
27	(`updateFolderMetadataAndPublish` / `replaceFileInFolder`) and update only the
28	Zustand store, bumping its IPNS `sequenceNumber` + child `modifiedAt`. The SDK
29	`folderTree` is never told → it goes stale.
30	
31	This was the root cause of PR #489's **deterministic** web-e2e failure
32	(recycle-bin TC08): after a file replace, a soft-delete via the stale `folderTree`
33	published at a stale sequence → 409 → the 409 merge's edit-beats-delete branch saw
34	`remote.modifiedAt > staleBase.modifiedAt` and **resurrected the just-deleted
35	file**. PR #489 shipped a reconciliation patch (`client.reconcileFolderState` +
36	`ensureFolderRegistered` calling it) — a band-aid that papers over the desync at
37	the SDK-mutation boundary, not a cure. A residual sub-second race remains (delete
38	firing between a replace's publish landing and its fire-and-forget `.then`
39	updating the store sequence), and any new direct-publish path can reintroduce the
40	bug.
41	
42	**Bypass-path inventory (the leak):**
43	
44	- `useFileOperations.updateFile` (file replace) — the fire-and-forget "6b" folder
45	  republish.
46	- `useFileVersions.handleRestoreVersion` and `handleDeleteVersion` (+ lazy
47	  IPNS-key migration).
48	
49	These call sdk-core publish helpers directly and update only the Zustand store.
50	
51	## Solution
52	
53	Make the **SDK client the single source of truth** for folder children +
54	`sequenceNumber`. The SDK is also consumed headless (desktop FUSE uses `client`
55	with no Zustand), so the SDK — not the store — must own state; making the SDK
56	stateless is ruled out.
57	
58	- Convert the bypass paths into client methods (e.g. `client.replaceFile()` /
59	  `client.updateFileMetadata()`) that own publish + sequence bookkeeping +
60	  `folder:updated` emission internally.
61	- Make the web `useFolderStore` a **projection**: keep UI/navigation state
62	  (`isLoaded`, `currentFolderId`, `breadcrumbs`, `parentId` tree) and a render
63	  copy of children, but write `children`/`sequenceNumber` **only** via
64	  `folder:updated` events — never from web mutation code.
65	- Aligns with the existing "gradual SDK adoption" bridge comment on
66	  `client.registerFolder`.
67	
68	**Exit criteria:** delete `client.reconcileFolderState` (becomes dead code — the
69	desync is impossible by construction), which also closes the residual race.
70	
71	**Scope:** medium, multi-PR refactor. Do NOT fold into the PR #489 bugfix.
72	
73	**References:** PR https://github.com/FSM1/cipher-box/pull/489; harness memory
74	`project-web-sdk-folder-state-desync`. Related but distinct todo:
75	`2026-06-14-unify-file-and-folder-ipns-cas-retry-into-one-publishwithcas.md`
76	(that one dedups the CAS *retry-loop code* in sdk-core; this one fixes folder
77	*state ownership* across the web/SDK boundary).
78	
```

> AGENT

Confirmed against the current code — yes, this todo **does** include web-app changes, and the bypass paths it names still exist exactly as written:

- `apps/web/src/hooks/useFileVersions.ts` imports and calls `replaceFileInFolder` + `updateFolderMetadataAndPublish` from `@cipherbox/sdk-core` directly (lines 106, 130, 247, 264).
- `apps/web/src/hooks/useFileOperations.ts` calls `updateFolderMetadataAndPublish` + `updateFileMetadata` directly (416, 458).
- `client.reconcileFolderState` (the band-aid) is still wired at `sdk-provider.ts:108`.
- The "good" pattern already exists: `useFolderMutations` routes create/delete/move/rename through `getSdkClient()` + `ensureFolderRegistered`, and `folder.store.ts:209` projects `folder:updated` events into Zustand.

So the web work this todo entails is specifically:
1. Convert the two leaking file hooks to new **client methods** (`client.replaceFile()` / `client.updateFileMetadata()`) that own publish + sequence bookkeeping + `folder:updated` emission internally — so the hooks no longer touch sdk-core folder-publish.
2. Make `useFolderStore` a **projection**: `children`/`sequenceNumber` written **only** via `folder:updated` events, never from web mutation code.
3. Delete `client.reconcileFolderState` (dead by construction → also closes the residual race).

## One important caveat on "no direct usage of sdk-core anywhere"

The goal is **single source of truth for folder state**, not "zero sdk-core imports in the web app." The todo only targets the paths that *mutate folder children/sequence* and update only Zustand. sdk-core stays a legitimate web dependency for things that don't touch folder state (crypto, raw uploads, file-metadata fetch/decrypt). So after this lands: the only **writer** of folder children/sequence in the web is `folder:updated` from the SDK client — but other sdk-core calls remain.

## A wording issue I should flag in the ROADMAP

My Phase 47 requirement (1) currently reads *"…reconcile `folderTree` before sdk-core publishes."* That actually describes the **band-aid** (`reconcileFolderState`), not the todo's real cure — which is to *eliminate* the bypass and *delete* `reconcileFolderState`. I'd like to correct it to:

> (1) Unify folder-state ownership so the web `useFolderStore` and the SDK client `folderTree` can't drift — route the two leaking web file hooks (`useFileOperations.updateFile`, `useFileVersions` restore/delete) through new SDK client methods, make `useFolderStore` a projection fed only by `folder:updated`, and delete the `reconcileFolderState` band-aid.

Want me to apply that ROADMAP correction?

Scope is folder-state-*mutating* paths only — non-mutating sdk-core usage in the web app (crypto, uploads, metadata fetch/decrypt) stays. > AGENT

Applied and pushed (`6c25f5c46`).

Phase 47 requirement (1) now reads as the single-source-of-truth cure — route the two leaking web file hooks (`useFileOperations.updateFile`, `useFileVersions` restore/delete) through new SDK client methods, make `useFolderStore` a projection fed only by `folder:updated`, and delete the `reconcileFolderState` band-aid — with the explicit caveat that scope is folder-state-**mutating** paths only (non-mutating sdk-core usage in the web stays).

The Phase 47 scope-todo bullet still points at `2026-06-14-unify-folder-state-ownership-in-sdk-client.md`, whose own Solution section already matches this — so the ROADMAP and the todo are now aligned for when you plan the phase.
